    buf8: ComputedBuffer
    buf8.layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1, s70, 2048], stride=[2048*s70, 2048, 1])

        NodeUser(node=ExternKernelSchedulerNode(name='op10'), can_inplace=False, is_weak=False),

addmm_34_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[s70, 2048], stride=[2048, 1])
buf8_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1, s70, 2048], stride=[2048*s70, 2048, 1])
class op8_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: s70, p1: 2048}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf5', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('addmm_34', get_index_1)
        neg = ops.neg(load_1)
        exp = ops.exp(neg)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        add = ops.add(exp, constant)
        reciprocal = ops.reciprocal(add)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(reciprocal, constant_1)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('addmm_34', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_3 = ops.load('addmm_34', get_index_3)
        neg_1 = ops.neg(load_3)
        exp_1 = ops.exp(neg_1)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(exp_1, constant_2)
        reciprocal_1 = ops.reciprocal(add_1)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(reciprocal_1, constant_3)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        sub = ops.sub(constant_4, mul_2)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(load_2, sub)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        add_2 = ops.add(mul_3, constant_5)
        mul_4 = ops.mul(mul_1, add_2)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf8', get_index_4, mul_4, None)
        return store

addmm_28_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[s70, 2048], stride=[2048, 1])
buf42_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1, s70, 2048], stride=[2048*s70, 2048, 1])
class op42_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: s70, p1: 2048}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf39', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('addmm_28', get_index_1)
        neg = ops.neg(load_1)
        exp = ops.exp(neg)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        add = ops.add(exp, constant)
        reciprocal = ops.reciprocal(add)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(reciprocal, constant_1)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('addmm_28', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_3 = ops.load('addmm_28', get_index_3)
        neg_1 = ops.neg(load_3)
        exp_1 = ops.exp(neg_1)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(exp_1, constant_2)
        reciprocal_1 = ops.reciprocal(add_1)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(reciprocal_1, constant_3)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        sub = ops.sub(constant_4, mul_2)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(load_2, sub)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        add_2 = ops.add(mul_3, constant_5)
        mul_4 = ops.mul(mul_1, add_2)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf42', get_index_4, mul_4, None)
        return store

buf73_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[s70, 2048], stride=[2048, 1])
addmm_22_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[s70, 2048], stride=[2048, 1])
buf76_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1, s70, 2048], stride=[2048*s70, 2048, 1])
class op76_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: s70, p1: 2048}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf73', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('addmm_22', get_index_1)
        neg = ops.neg(load_1)
        exp = ops.exp(neg)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        add = ops.add(exp, constant)
        reciprocal = ops.reciprocal(add)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(reciprocal, constant_1)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_2 = ops.load('addmm_22', get_index_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_3 = ops.load('addmm_22', get_index_3)
        neg_1 = ops.neg(load_3)
        exp_1 = ops.exp(neg_1)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(exp_1, constant_2)
        reciprocal_1 = ops.reciprocal(add_1)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(reciprocal_1, constant_3)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        sub = ops.sub(constant_4, mul_2)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(load_2, sub)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        add_2 = ops.add(mul_3, constant_5)
        mul_4 = ops.mul(mul_1, add_2)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf76', get_index_4, mul_4, None)
        return store
